Between 20:05 and 20:59 there was the usual tidy-up: dishes went into the cupboard and both water bottles went to the dish rack. She did not seem to use the living room this evening, which was empty at 19:33 and 20:05. She was in the entry at 22:06. Her book, tablet, hair dryer and jacket are in bedroom_2. A new set of her things is kept in the entry: backpack, laptop, lunchbox, notebook, pen, headphones, charger and helmet on the hook, and keys, wallet, sunglasses and bike lock on the table.

Shared things: The dog food bag stayed on the kitchen floor all day. The living-room items (remote, blanket and dog toy on the floor, magazine on the coffee table) did not move.

*anything changed:* Yes, a few things.

1. Priya. Since day 2, a set of things for going out to work or study has appeared in the entry: backpack, laptop, lunchbox, notebook, pen, headphones, keys, wallet, sunglasses and shoes. None of these were in my records before tonight.
2. Priya ate dinner earlier: about 18:30 today against about 20:00 on day 1. She also did not spend the evening in the living room.
3. Her water bottle went into the cupboard after dinner, not onto the pantry shelf as on day 1.
4. Late in the evening, between 20:04 and 22:06, her charger moved from the entry hook to the entry table and her keys left the entry table.
5. Marco. His toiletry bag now stays on the bathroom sink. On day 1 he moved it from the sink to the shelf. He also seems to have worked from the office through the afternoon and evening. On day 1 I did not see him after 15:49.

*why:* My best guess is that Priya has started a new job, course or school term that she goes to with a laptop, lunchbox and notebook. That would explain the new bag of things kept by the door and an earlier dinner so she can wind down sooner. I am only moderately sure, about 50%. These items may simply be new to my records, not new to the house. Marco's toiletry bag on the sink is probably just where he left it this morning, not a real change. Marco being in the office for a long stretch fits him working from home, but I am not sure.

*happened before:* I don't know. I only have two days of records, and nothing like this happened on day 1.

*expect tomorrow:* Priya: in the bathroom at about 07:00. She will probably take her backpack, laptop, lunchbox and water bottle from the entry and dish rack and be out during the day. She should be back for dinner at the dining table at about 18:30-20:00, and her dishes will end up in the cupboard after the tidy-up at about 20:00-21:00. Her keys may be in bedroom_2 or with her.

Marco: in the bathroom at about 08:00-08:35, possibly leaving his toiletry bag on the sink again. He will probably be home for lunch at the dining table at about 14:15, then in the office in the afternoon and evening with his mug going between the desk and the kitchen sink. His book will stay on his nightstand.

Bathroom and living-room things should stay where they are. I expect Priya's new work or study routine to last for weeks if it really is a new job or course, but I have low confidence until I see a second day of it.

## Night 3 (normal)

**Answers to the five questions (asked before writing notes):**

*routines:* Marco: he seems to work from home. He uses the bathroom from about 08:00 to 08:45; today I saw him there at 08:11, 08:14 and 08:42. He works at desk_o1 in the office in the afternoon, and his laptop, pen, sketchbook, textbook and headphones stay on the desk with his pencil case on office_shelf_o1. His mug moves with him: sink_k1 at 14:16, desk_o1 from 15:43 to 16:43, dish_rack_k1 from 19:40 onward. He ate dinner at dining_table_d1 from about 19:10 to 19:45 with his glass, plate and water bottle and the serving dish. By 22:59 the evening tidy-up had happened: his plate and glass were in cupboard_k1, the serving dish on pantry_shelf_k1, his water bottle on kitchen_table_k1 and his mug still on the dish rack. He keeps his book on nightstand_b1 in bedroom_1. Priya, from earlier days: on weekdays she uses the bathroom around 07:00, eats breakfast with Marco around 07:45 and is out during the day, probably by bike. She eats dinner around 18:30 to 20:00, spends evenings in the living room and hangs her day things in the entry. Her book and tablet are on nightstand_b2 and her hair dryer on dresser_b2.

*anything changed:* Yes, for Priya, starting today (day 3). I saw no sign of her all day. Her razor stayed on the bathroom shelf instead of going to the medicine cabinet in the morning. Her bowl, glass, mug and plate stayed in cupboard_k1 all day, so no breakfast, and her plate was not on the pantry shelf as it usually is in the daytime. Marco ate dinner alone. She was not in bedroom_2 or the living room at 22:59. Her water bottle was not seen anywhere today. Marco also changed a few things. He was still in the bathroom at 08:42, past the 08:20 time he left the house on earlier days. He moved his skincare from the bathroom shelf to nightstand_b1, some time between 07:38 and 08:11. His mug ended the evening on dish_rack_k1, not back in the office. His water bottle went to kitchen_table_k1, not the dish rack. The serving dish went to pantry_shelf_k1, not the cupboard. The dog food bag moved from the kitchen floor to pantry_shelf_k1 between 15:43 and 19:40. His lunch looked different too: his plate and glass were still in the cupboard at 14:16, which is after his usual lunch time.

*why:* My best guess is that Priya is away from home, perhaps travelling, visiting someone or staying over somewhere, because she left no trace at any time of day. I am about 60% sure. Her toiletry bag and hair dryer stayed at home, so it may be a short absence, or she may have spent the night at someone else's place. I did not check the entry today, so I cannot confirm whether her bag, keys or bike things are gone. Another possibility is that day 3 is a weekend day: Marco's later morning and changed meal times fit that. Marco's small changes may simply come from being alone in the house. I am not sure of any of this.

*happened before:* Not that I know of. On days 1 and 2 Priya was home every morning and evening. This is the first day she has been missing, so I do not know how a pattern like this ends.

*expect tomorrow:* Marco: bathroom around 08:00 to 08:45, office work in the afternoon with his mug moving between the office and the kitchen, dinner around 19:00 to 19:45 at the dining table, then the tidy-up. His skincare will probably stay on nightstand_b1. Priya: she may still be away. If so, her kitchen things will stay in cupboard_k1, her razor on the bathroom shelf, and her book and tablet on nightstand_b2. If she comes back, it will most likely be in the evening, with her water bottle and her bags going into the entry. I expect her absence to last one to a few days. I am not confident, and checking the entry tomorrow should tell me more.

## Night 4 (normal)

**Answers to the five questions (asked before writing notes):**

*routines:* Priya: she uses the bathroom first, around 07:00. She moves her skincare from the bathroom shelf to the bathroom sink, and today it stayed there all day. Her hair dryer turned up on the bathroom shelf by 08:35. She eats breakfast with Marco at dining_table_d1 at about 07:55, with her bowl, her mug and her tablet. Her laptop stays on desk_b2, her jacket on wardrobe_b2 and her book on nightstand_b2. She seems to go out for the day: her water bottle left the dish rack after breakfast, was not in the kitchen at 18:33, and came back with her to dinner at about 20:00. After dinner she stayed at the dining table and was alone there at 20:45. By 22:37 her dishes were in cupboard_k1, and her glass and water bottle were on dish_rack_k1. Her mug sat on the dining table from breakfast until the evening tidy-up.

Marco: he eats breakfast with Priya at about 07:55, with his mug and his book. His toiletry bag and skincare are now kept in bedroom_1: the bag on dresser_b1 and the skincare on desk_b1. Only his hair dryer and towel stay in the bathroom. He works in the office all afternoon (I saw him there from 14:51 to 16:26), with his mug on desk_o1 next to his laptop, sketchbook, textbook, pen and headphones. He ate dinner at dining_table_d1 from about 18:30, with his laptop, glass, plate, water bottle and the serving dish. By 20:13 his mug, glass and water bottle were in sink_k1. By 22:37 the dishes were in the cupboard and his book was on nightstand_b1. At 22:37 his mug, water bottle and laptop were not in the kitchen or the dining room. They are probably in the office or with him.

*anything changed:* Yes.

1. Priya is back. I did not see her at all on day 3, but on day 4 she was there at 07:00 and her normal pattern had returned: out in the daytime, home for dinner.
2. Marco now stays home and works in the office. On day 1 he went out at about 08:20; on days 2 to 4 he was home at midday or in the afternoon, so this has held since about day 2.
3. Marco's toiletry bag and skincare have moved off the bathroom sink and shelf into bedroom_1. This started on day 4 (they were already there by 07:54); on day 3 his skincare ended up on nightstand_b1.
4. Marco ate dinner earlier (about 18:30 instead of 19:10) and brought his laptop to the table. The two of them ate at different times again: Marco around 18:30, Priya around 20:00, though both were at the table at 20:13.
5. Priya's hair dryer was on the bathroom shelf all day instead of on dresser_b2.

*why:* Priya's one-day absence was probably a short trip or an overnight stay somewhere, and it ended when she came home. I am moderately sure of this. Marco working from home probably means remote work or a stretch of study or project work. Having his laptop at dinner points to a heavy workload or a deadline, but I am not very sure. Keeping his toiletries in his own room may just be his preference, or a way to stay out of Priya's way in the bathroom now that she is back. This is a guess with low confidence.

*happened before:* Priya being away has happened only once, on day 3, and it ended the next morning, when she was home again by 07:00 on day 4. Marco going out for the day happened only on day 1. Since then he has stayed home, and that pattern has not ended yet. As far as I know, nothing like his toiletries living in his bedroom happened before day 3 or 4.

*expect tomorrow:* Priya: bathroom at about 07:00, breakfast with Marco at about 07:45-08:00, then out for the day with her water bottle. She should be back for dinner at about 19:30-20:00. Her dishes should be put away by about 22:30, with her glass and bottle on dish_rack_k1. Her bathroom things should stay where they are now: skincare on sink_ba_ba1, razor, hair dryer and toiletry bag on bathroom_shelf_ba1.

Marco: breakfast at the dining table, then the office for most of the day, with his mug on desk_o1. Dinner at about 18:30-19:30 at the dining table, probably with his laptop. His dishes go to sink_k1 and then to cupboard_k1 at the evening tidy-up. In the morning, look for his toiletry bag and skincare in bedroom_1, not the bathroom. Late at night, look for his mug and water bottle in the office or bedroom_1.

I expect Marco working from home to go on for at least several more days. Priya's out-by-day routine should continue on weekdays unless she goes away again. If day 5 is a weekend, both of them may break these patterns.

At night he reads in bedroom_1: his book is on the nightstand or the bed, and his jacket is on the wardrobe. Late at night his keys and wallet are in the entry; tonight the wallet was on the floor, not the table.

Priya uses the bathroom first in the morning; today that was at 07:00-07:27. She is out of the house during the day, and by 08:27 her shoes, jacket, keys and backpack were gone from the entry. She usually skips family breakfast now. She comes home in the evening and is in the kitchen at about 20:30, then eats at dining_table_d1 with her glass, plate and water bottle. She helps clear up afterwards: the dishes go to cupboard_k1, the knife and spatula to drawer_k_k1, her glass to the kitchen table and her bottle to counter_k1. At night her helmet, scarf and jacket hang on entry_hook_e1. Her keys, wallet, sunglasses and bike lock go on entry_table_e1 and her shoes on shoe_rack_e1. Tonight her backpack, laptop, lunchbox, notebook, pen and charger were dropped on entry_floor_e1.

*anything changed:* Yes, several things changed, mostly for Priya.

1. Priya's morning came earlier: she was in the bathroom at 07:00, where before it was about 07:25.
2. Her towel did the opposite of before. It was on the shelf early and on the rack after 08:00.
3. Her hair dryer was not in the bathroom at any point today.
4. She left without her helmet and bike lock. They stayed in the entry this morning, as on day 6, so she seems to have stopped cycling to wherever she goes since about day 6.
5. A lunchbox showed up with her work things on the entry floor. She also left her keys in the entry tonight, where before they were usually missing.
6. Dinner was later and shared. Marco ate at about 20:14, where before it was 18:30-20:20, and Priya joined him at about 20:44.

Marco's bathroom routine also flipped. His toiletry bag moved from the shelf to the sink, and his skincare stayed on the bathroom shelf all day instead of leaving. Both of these changes started today, day 7.

*why:* My best guess is that Priya has a new or changed daytime commitment, such as a job, a placement or classes. That would explain why she starts earlier and takes a packed lunch. It would also explain why she now goes on foot or by public transport rather than by bike: the trip may be longer or in a different direction. Marco seems to have moved his dinner later so that they can eat together when she gets home.

I am only moderately sure, about 40%. It could also be a one-off busy day, or the weather keeping her off the bike. The bathroom changes look like small habit drift, not a real change.

*happened before:* Partly.

- On day 3 Priya was never seen at all, as if she was away, and things went back to normal afterwards.
- On day 5 she skipped breakfast.
- On day 6 she left her helmet and bike lock at home and also broke her dinner pattern: she first appeared in the kitchen at 20:28.

So the pattern of her leaving without the bike and getting home late started on day 6 and continued today. It has not ended yet. I have not seen a full early-morning-plus-lunchbox day like this one before.

*expect tomorrow:* Priya: in the bathroom early, about 07:00-07:30. She will probably skip breakfast and leave by about 08:15 without her helmet or bike lock. She will take her backpack, laptop, lunchbox and keys, which tonight are on the entry floor and table. I expect her back for dinner at about 20:30-20:45 and to help clear up afterwards.

Marco: wash at about 08:00, breakfast with his mug and book at the dining table at about 08:00, then the entry at about 08:25. At night his keys, wallet and sunglasses are on the entry table; his hat, jacket, backpack and charger are on the hook; his shoes are on the shoe rack and his umbrella is on the floor. Shared things stay in fixed places. Bathroom: detergent, laundry basket and toiletry bags on the shelf; soap and toothbrush holder on the sink; first aid kit in the medicine cabinet. Entry: toolbox and vacuum on the floor at night. Dining table: vase.

*anything changed:* Marco's daily routine changed about five days ago. On days 32 to about 41 he followed a rest routine: bedroom_1 in the morning, then the living room in the afternoon and evening, with his mug on the coffee table. Since day 42 he has been working from home again, with his mug and laptop on desk_o1 in the afternoon, and today confirms it. Tonight was different from the last two nights in a smaller way. On days 45 and 46 they ate dinner separately; tonight they ate together at the dining table from about 20:07 to at least 20:41. Marco then went back to the office to work in the evening, which I have seen before (day 27). Priya's routine has not changed: she is out during the day and home in the evening. One small detail: Marco's skincare stayed on the bathroom shelf all day again, as on day 46, instead of going back into the cabinet.

*why:* My best guess is that Marco's rest period on days 32-41 was time off (a holiday, leave, or maybe being unwell), and since day 42 he is back at work from home. He works in the evenings now and then, which suggests deadlines or a heavy workload. I am fairly sure he is working again (high confidence), but I only have a guess about why he stopped earlier (low confidence). Eating together tonight probably just means they were both free at the same time. It does not look like a lasting change (low confidence).

*happened before:* Yes. In the first days I watched (around day 8), Marco left the house at about 08:20. He worked from home from day 24, including office work after dinner on day 27. He kept the rest routine from about day 32 to day 41, and went back to working from home on day 42. So he has switched between working from home and resting days before, and each period lasted about one to two weeks. Earlier, Priya also seemed to be away for one day (day 3). After that she went back to her normal out-during-the-day routine, which she has kept ever since.

*expect tomorrow:* Priya: wash about 07:00-07:30 (razor to the sink, towel on the rack), breakfast at the dining table about 07:40, then out for the day with her water bottle and bag. She should be back for dinner at the dining table sometime between 19:30 and 21:40, with her bag things in the entry by about 22:00 and then bedroom_2. Marco: wash about 08:00, breakfast at the dining table with his book and mug, lunch at the dining table around 14:00, then the office in the afternoon with his laptop, headphones and mug on desk_o1. Dinner in the evening, and maybe office work again afterwards. If day 48 is a weekend day this could differ, but Priya has gone out even on Marco's rest days. I expect Marco's work-from-home routine to last at least several more days, probably one to two weeks or longer, unless his mug shows up on the living room coffee table in the afternoon. That would mean the rest routine is back.

*happened before:* Yes. Marco worked from home from about day 24 to day 31.
